Method: each file in the Rocket.Chat 7.9.1 tree (excluding apps/meteor/ee/ and ee/,
556 files) was compared by SHA-256 with the same path in PrivOS Hub at commit
8f5f5a7f2. Of 9,591 upstream files: 7456 are byte-identical (listed below), 1,283
are modified (PrivOS Community License applies to the modifications), 203 are not
present in PrivOS Hub. Regenerate at each release.
